# API list

> **Every operation in the package, and which app calls it.**
> Generated from `contracts/` by `tools/build-api-list.py` — **do not edit this file.**
> An operation that is in a contract is published; there is no other state for it to be in.

**2,669 operations across 33 contracts** — 1,015 in the spine, 1,654 in satellites.

`Offline` is `x-ticvai-offline-capable`: whether the operation may be served from a device that cannot reach the platform. `Consumers` is `x-ticvai-consumed-by`, written back into the contracts by `tools/link-screens-contracts.py` from the screens that declare the operation — so an operation with no consumer is one no screen has been drawn for yet.

---
